# Skills · O · part 20

866 entries, on-anger to onboard-brand. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **on-anger**: Apply Seneca's framework from On Anger, anger is brief madness, and the best remedy is delay. Use when the user is triggered by a message, a meeting, or a person and about to respond from the trigger rather than from considered judgment. Also useful when designing protocols (personal or team) that put time between provocation and reaction. Sourced from De Ira (On Anger) by Seneca, Book I §§ 1-7 (the diagnosis) and Book II §§ 28-29 (the daily examination). ([adamtpang/summon.guide](https://github.com/adamtpang/summon.guide/tree/HEAD/plugins/seneca/skills/on-anger))

The `on-call` script polls and acks without the model and runs `--triage` once per page; `/loop` still works but burns tokens. TRIGGER when: user says "on-call", "start on-call", "watch the pager", "go on call", or a /loop run invokes it.
